Like they leave little marks or signs on the tickets to show I messed up… or to get me fired.\n\nMy husband says it’s just normal restaurant mix-ups, but it doesn’t really feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like certain people are sending you hidden messages or trying to harm you in some way, beyond just watching your work mistakes?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about harm exactly… but it feels like more than just watching. Like a couple servers and one regular leave little signs on the tickets, like they’re trying to prove I messed up so I’ll get fired. My husband says it’s just normal restaurant stuff, but… I can’t shake it.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your actions or thoughts are being controlled by something outside of yourself, like someone else is making you do or say things?\n\nPatient: Not really like… being controlled. It’s more that I feel like people at work are watching what I do, like how I ring things in. A couple servers, and one regular… I keep thinking they’re leaving signs on the tickets to show I messed up, like they want me fired. My husband says it’s just normal mistakes, but it doesn’t feel that way.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like things happening around you—like things people say, or events on TV or the radio—are directed at you specifically, even if others don’t see it that way?\n\nPatient: Not really TV or radio… I don’t think so. It’s more at work. Like the way some tickets come back, or little marks on them… it feels like they’re meant for me, like the servers or that regular are trying to show I messed up. My husband says I’m reading into it, but it doesn’t feel random.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like things people say or do in public, like on TV, in conversations, or in other places, are meant specifically for you, even if others don’t seem to notice it that way?\n\nPatient: Not really TV or anything like that. It’s mostly at the restaurant… like some of the servers, and one regular, I feel like they’re watching how I ring things in. Sometimes the tickets have little marks or notes and it feels like they’re trying to show I messed up, like to get me fired. My husband says it’s just normal stuff, but… I don’t know.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I hear a woman say my name, or say I’m counting wrong. Like at closing, or when I’m in my kitchen at home. It’s clear enough that I’ll answer, and then… I realize no one else heard it.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you believed something strongly that others around you didn’t seem to understand or agree with, even when they said it wasn’t true?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess. At work I keep thinking a couple of the servers, and this one regular, are watching how I ring things in. Like they leave little marks or signs on the tickets to show I messed up… or to get me fired.\n\nMy husband says it’s just normal restaurant mix-ups, but it doesn’t really feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like certain people are sending you hidden messages or trying to harm you in some way, beyond just watching your work mistakes?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about harm exactly… but it feels like more than just watching. My husband says it’s just normal restaurant stuff, but… I can’t shake it.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your actions or thoughts are being controlled by something outside of yourself, like someone else is making you do or say things?\n\nPatient: Not really like… being controlled. It’s more at work. Like the way some tickets come back, or little marks on them… it feels like they’re meant for me, like the servers or that regular are trying to show I messed up. My husband says I’m reading into it, but it doesn’t feel random.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like things people say or do in public, like on TV, in conversations, or in other places, are meant specifically for you, even if others don’t seem to notice it that way?\n\nPatient: Not really TV or anything like that.
